     1→# NEXUS//SIGNALS — Worklog
     2→
     3→Project: Full redesign → "NEXUS//SIGNALS" — AI crypto trading signal command terminal.
     4→Stack: Next.js 16 App Router + TS + Tailwind 4 + shadcn/ui + Prisma/SQLite + TanStack Query + Zustand + Framer Motion + Recharts.
     5→Design: Cyber-Terminal × Neo-Brutalism Bento × Dark Glassmorphism. Palette: neon mint #00ffa3 (primary), violet #b26bff, magenta #ff4ecd, amber #ffb347, danger #ff4d5e, cyan #45f0df. Fonts: Space Grotesk (display), JetBrains Mono (terminal). Always dark.

    13→Work Log:
    14→- Created prisma/schema.prisma: Signal (entry/zone, tp1-3, sl, leverage, margin, confidence, RR, timeframe, strategy, status, pnl, ttl, expiresAt, analysis, tags), SignalEvent (typed feed), MarketAsset (price/sparkline JSON/volume), PricePoint (48h history). db:push OK.
    15→- prisma/seed.ts: 10 assets, 40 signals (34 resolved across 7d + 6 active), 76 events, 480 price points. Seeded OK.
    16→- src/lib/engine.ts: tick() throttled 3.5s — random-walk prices, sparkline update, TP1/TP2/TP3/SL/TTL resolution with events, auto-spawn signals (max 5 active), flavor terminal events.
    17→- src/lib/stats.ts: buildStats() — winRate, pnlUSD (margin*pnl%), equity curve by day, bySymbol/Timeframe/Strategy, confidence buckets, streak, best/worst.
    18→- src/lib/format.ts + src/lib/api-types.ts: shared DTOs & formatters (client-safe).
    19→- APIs: GET /api/pulse (tick+snapshot: market, signals, events, stats), GET/POST /api/signals, GET/PATCH /api/signals/[id] (close), GET /api/events, POST /api/ai/chat + POST /api/ai/analyze (z-ai-web-dev-sdk LLM, server-only, fallback if offline).
    20→- Verified via curl: /api/pulse and /api/signals return live data. Engine ticking.

   157→- BUG FOUND (critical, data drift): after ~8h runtime, total PnL exploded to $114K — engine.ts TP3 final pnl used `riskReward / slDist * 100` (same bug as seed, fixed in R1 seed but engine copy was missed). FIXED: finalPnl = rr × slDist × leverage × 100 (consistent with live-pnl formula). Wrote scripts/repair-pnl.ts — recomputed 7 corrupted rows → totals realistic again ($4.1K, max +369%, min -151%).
